headerView.findViewById(R.id.diskUsage);\n\n        String defaultValue = getString(R.string.pref_header_unit_default_value);\n        String s = null;\n        String nav_header_unit = preferences.getString("nav_header_unit", defaultValue);\n        if (nav_header_unit.equals(getString(R.string.pref_header_unit_m_value))) {\n            s = DiskUtil.freeSpaceMebi(true) + " MiB " + getString(R.string.free);\n        } else if (nav_header_unit.equals(getString(R.string.pref_header_unit_g_value))) {\n            s = DiskUtil.freeSpaceGibi(true) + " GiB " + getString(R.string.free);\n        }\n        if (s == null) {\n            Log.e(TAG, "Unable to get free space! requested: " + nav_header_unit);\n        }\n        diskUsageTextView.setText(s);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Loads the bookmarks into the menu.\n     */\n    private void loadUserBookmarks() {\n        Menu menu = navigationView.getMenu();\n        TreeSet<String> bookmarks;\n        if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= android.os.Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {\n            bookmarks = new TreeSet<>(Comparator.comparing(this::getTitleFromPath));\n        } else { // FUCK THEM PUNY OLD VERSION USERS!\n            //noinspection ComparatorCombinators\n            bookmarks = new TreeSet<>((o1, o2) -> getTitleFromPath(o1).compareTo(getTitleFromPath(o2)));\n        }\n        bookmarks.addAll(new PrefUtils<HashSet<String>>(BOOKMARKS).getValue());\n        if (!bookmarks.isEmpty()) {\n            bookmarkItems = new HashSet<>();\n            menu.removeGroup(R.id.bookmarksMenuGroup);\n            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {\n                bookmarks.forEach((s) -> addBookmarkToMenu(menu, s, bookmarks));\n            } else { // also fuck all of those vendors that don\'t update. You are making development ugly!\n                for (String s : bookmarks) {\n                    addBookmarkToMenu(menu, s, bookmarks);\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            Log.d(TAG, "User has no bookmarks");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a bookmark to the navigation drawer.\n     *\n     * @param menu      the menu\n     * @param s         the bookmark\'s display text\n     * @param bookmarks the bookmarks\n     */\n    private void addBookmarkToMenu(Menu menu, String s, Set<String> bookmarks) {\n        String title = getTitleFromPath(s);\n        MenuItem item = menu.add(R.id.bookmarksMenuGroup, Menu.NONE, 2, title);\n        item.setIcon(R.drawable.ic_bookmark_border_black_24dp);\n        Bookmark bookmark = new Bookmark(s, title, item);\n        if (new PrefUtils<Boolean>(BOOKMARK_EDIT_MODE).getValue()) {\n            item.setActionView(R.layout.editable_menu_item);\n            View v = bookmarks, item, bookmark));\n            ImageButton editButton = v.findViewById(R.id.menu_item_action_edit);\n            editButton.setOnClickListener((v1) -> {\n                AlertDialog dia = new AlertDialog.Builder(this)\n                        .setView(R.layout.layout_path_prompt)\n                        .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, (dialog, which) -> Log.d(TAG, "Operation addBookmarkToMenu(menu, ((EditText) dia.findViewById(R.id.destinationPath)).getText().toString(), bookmarks);\n                });\n                dia.show();\n            });\n        }\n        bookmarkItems.add(bookmark);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the last part of a path.\n     *\n     * @param s absolute path\n     * @return last item of path\n     */\n    public String getTitleFromPath(String s) {\n        if (s != null) {\n            if (!s.equals(ROOT_DIR)) {\n                String[] split = s.split("/");\n                int i = split.length - 1;\n                if (i < 0) {\n                    i = 0;\n                }\n                return split[i];\n            } else {\n                return s;\n            }\n        } else {\n            return "null";\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Removes a bookmark.\n     *\n     * @param menu      the bookmarks menu\n     * @param s         entry to remove\n     * @param bookmarks the bookmarks\n     * @param item      the menu item to remove\n     * @param bookmark  the bookmark to remove\n     */\n    @SuppressLint("ApplySharedPref")\n    private void removeBookmarkFromMenu(Menu menu, String s, Set<String> bookmarks, MenuItem item, Bookmark bookmark) {\n        menu.removeItem(item.getItemId());\n        bookmarkItems.remove(bookmark);\n        bookmarks.remove(s);\n        new PrefUtils<Set<String>>(BOOKMARKS).setValue(bookmarks);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Loads home dir selected by user.\n     */\n    @SuppressLint("UseSparseArrays")\n    private void loadHomeDir() {\n        historyMap = new HashMap<>();\n        historyCounter = 0;\n        String absolutePath = Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory().getAbsolutePath();\n        PrefUtils<String> homeDirPreference = new PrefUtils<>(HOME_DIR);\n        String startDir = homeDirPreference.getValue();\n        if (startDir == null) {\n            homeDirPreference.setValue(absolutePath);\n            startDir = absolutePath;\n        }\n        loadPath(startDir);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * F5\n     * <p>\n     * Keeps the current scroll position since the content of the list doesn\'t change (much)\n     * when simply reloading the directory that is already displayed.\n     */\n    public void reloadCurrentDirectory() {\n        saveScrollPosition();\n        loadPath(currentDirectory);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Saves the current scroll position of the {@link #fileListView} so it can be restored\n     * after the list has been (re-)populated.\n     *\n     * @see #restoreScrollPosition()\n     */\n    private void saveScrollPosition() {\n        if (fileListView != null) {\n            scrollPosition = fileListView.getFirstVisiblePosition();\n            View firstVisibleItem = fileListView.getChildAt(0);\n            scrollOffset = (firstVisibleItem == null) ? 0 : (firstVisibleItem.getTop() - fileListView.getPaddingTop());\n            scrollPositionSaved = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Restores a previously saved scroll position of the {@link #fileListView}.\n     * Does nothing if no scroll position has been saved.\n     *\n     * @see #saveScrollPosition()\n     */\n    private void restoreScrollPosition() {\n        if (scrollPositionSaved) {\n            scrollPositionSaved = false; // only restore once per saved position\n            if (fileListView != null) {\n                final int position = scrollPosition;\n                final int offset = scrollOffset;\n                fileListView.setSelectionFromTop(position, offset);\n                // the adapter has just been set, so the list may not be laid out yet\n                fileListView.post(() -> fileListView.setSelectionFromTop(position, offset));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")\n    // one of the two methods that may call the "deprecated" functions.\n    public void loadArchivePath(String path, FMArchive archive) {\n        loadArchive(path, archive);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Changes directory.\n     *\n     * @param path the path to cd into\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")\n    // one of the two methods that may call the "deprecated" functions.\n    public void loadPath(String path) {\n        ArchiveParentFinder archiveResult = new ArchiveParentFinder(path).invoke();\n        boolean archive = archiveResult.isArchive();\n        FMArchive aF = archiveResult.getArchiveFile();\n        if (archive) {\n            loadArchive(path, aF);\n        } else {\n            loadDirectory(path);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Changes directory.\n     *\n     * @param path directory to load\n     * @deprecated may only be called by {@link #loadPath(String)}\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings("DeprecatedIsStillUsed") // see above\n    private void loadDirectory(String path) {\n        ArrayList<FMFile> files;\n        FileLoader fileLoader = new FileLoader(path);\n        View errorText = findViewById(R.id.unableToLoadText);\n        View emptyText = findViewById(R.id.emptyDirText);\n        try {\n            files = fileLoader.loadLocationFiles();\n            fileListView.setVisibility(VISIBLE);\n            errorText.setVisibility(GONE);\n            emptyText.setVisibility(GONE);\n\n            arrayAdapter = new FileArrayAdapter(this, R.layout.layout_file, sortFilesByPreference(files, new PrefUtils<String>(SORT_FILES_BY).getValue()));\n            fileListView.setAdapter(arrayAdapter);\n            restoreScrollPosition();\n        } catch (NoAccessException e) {\n            Log.w(TAG, "Can\'t read \'" + path + "\': Permission denied!");\n            fileListView.setVisibility(GONE);\n            errorText.setVisibility(VISIBLE);\n            emptyText.setVisibility(GONE);\n        } catch (EmptyDirectoryException e) {\n            Log.w(TAG, "Can\'t read \'" + path + "\': Empty directory!");\n            fileListView.setVisibility(GONE);\n            errorText.setVisibility(GONE);\n            emptyText.setVisibility(VISIBLE);\n        }\n        currentDirectory = path;\n\n        HistoryEntry entry = historyMap.get(historyCounter);\n        if (entry != null && !entry.getPath().equals(currentDirectory)) {\n            historyMap.put(historyCounter++, new HistoryEntry(currentDirectory, null));\n        }\n        setToolbarText();\n        setFreeSpaceText();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Changes "directory" into an archive.\n     *\n     * @param path archive to load\n     * @deprecated may only be called by {@link #loadPath(String)}\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings("DeprecatedIsStillUsed") // see above\n    private void loadArchive(String path, FMArchive archive) {\n        ArrayList<FMFile> files;\n        ArchiveLoader loader = new ArchiveLoader(archive, path);\n        View errorText = findViewById(R.id.unableToLoadText);\n        View emptyText = findViewById(R.id.emptyDirText);\n\n        files = loader.loadLocationFiles();\n        fileListView.setVisibility(VISIBLE);\n        errorText.setVisibility(GONE);\n        emptyText.setVisibility(GONE);\n\n        arrayAdapter = new ArchiveArrayAdapter(this, R.layout.layout_file, sortFilesByPreference(files, new PrefUtils<String>(SORT_FILES_BY).getValue()), archive);\n        fileListView.setAdapter(arrayAdapter);\n\n        currentDirectory = path;\n        historyMap.put(historyCounter++, new HistoryEntry(currentDirectory, archive));\n        setToolbarText();\n        setFreeSpaceText();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Sorts files by user preference.\n     *\n     * @param files files to sort\n     * @param pref  chosen preference\n     * @return sorted list\n headerView.findViewById(R.id.diskUsage);\n\n        String defaultValue = getString(R.string.pref_header_unit_default_value);\n        String s = null;\n        String nav_header_unit = preferences.getString("nav_header_unit", defaultValue);\n        if (nav_header_unit.equals(getString(R.string.pref_header_unit_m_value))) {\n            s = DiskUtil.freeSpaceMebi(true) + " MiB " + getString(R.string.free);\n        } else if (nav_header_unit.equals(getString(R.string.pref_header_unit_g_value))) {\n            s = DiskUtil.freeSpaceGibi(true) + " GiB " + getString(R.string.free);\n        }\n        if (s == null) {\n            Log.e(TAG, "Unable to get free space! requested: " + nav_header_unit);\n        }\n        diskUsageTextView.setText(s);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Loads the bookmarks into the menu.\n     */\n    private void loadUserBookmarks() {\n        Menu menu = navigationView.getMenu();\n        TreeSet<String> bookmarks;\n        if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= android.os.Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {\n            bookmarks = new TreeSet<>(Comparator.comparing(this::getTitleFromPath));\n        } else { // FUCK THEM PUNY OLD VERSION USERS!\n            //noinspection ComparatorCombinators\n            bookmarks = new TreeSet<>((o1, o2) -> getTitleFromPath(o1).compareTo(getTitleFromPath(o2)));\n        }\n        bookmarks.addAll(new PrefUtils<HashSet<String>>(BOOKMARKS).getValue());\n        if (!bookmarks.isEmpty()) {\n            bookmarkItems = new HashSet<>();\n            menu.removeGroup(R.id.bookmarksMenuGroup);\n            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {\n                bookmarks.forEach((s) -> addBookmarkToMenu(menu, s, bookmarks));\n            } else { // also fuck all of those vendors that don\'t update. You are making development ugly!\n                for (String s : bookmarks) {\n                    addBookmarkToMenu(menu, s, bookmarks);\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            Log.d(TAG, "User has no bookmarks");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a bookmark to the navigation drawer.\n     *\n     * @param menu      the menu\n     * @param s         the bookmark\'s display text\n     * @param bookmarks the bookmarks\n     */\n    private void addBookmarkToMenu(Menu menu, String s, Set<String> bookmarks) {\n        String title = getTitleFromPath(s);\n        MenuItem item = menu.add(R.id.bookmarksMenuGroup, Menu.NONE, 2, title);\n        item.setIcon(R.drawable.ic_bookmark_border_black_24dp);\n        Bookmark bookmark = new Bookmark(s, title, item);\n        if (new PrefUtils<Boolean>(BOOKMARK_EDIT_MODE).getValue()) {\n            item.setActionView(R.layout.editable_menu_item);\n            View v = bookmarks, item, bookmark));\n            ImageButton editButton = v.findViewById(R.id.menu_item_action_edit);\n            editButton.setOnClickListener((v1) -> {\n                AlertDialog dia = addBookmarkToMenu(menu, ((EditText) dia.findViewById(R.id.destinationPath)).getText().toString(), bookmarks);\n                });\n                dia.show();\n            });\n        }\n        bookmarkItems.add(bookmark);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the last part of a path.\n     *\n     * @param s absolute path\n     * @return last item of path\n     */\n    public String getTitleFromPath(String s) {\n        if (s != null) {\n            if (!s.equals(ROOT_DIR)) {\n                String[] split = s.split("/");\n                int i = split.length - 1;\n                if (i < 0) {\n                    i = 0;\n                }\n                return split[i];\n            } else {\n                return s;\n            }\n        } else {\n            return "null";\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Removes a bookmark.\n     *\n     * @param menu      the bookmarks menu\n     * @param s         entry to remove\n     * @param bookmarks the bookmarks\n     * @param item      the menu item to remove\n     * @param bookmark  the bookmark to remove\n     */\n    @SuppressLint("ApplySharedPref")\n    private void removeBookmarkFromMenu(Menu menu, String s, Set<String> bookmarks, MenuItem item, Bookmark bookmark) {\n        menu.removeItem(item.getItemId());\n        bookmarkItems.remove(bookmark);\n        bookmarks.remove(s);\n        new PrefUtils<Set<String>>(BOOKMARKS).setValue(bookmarks);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Loads home dir selected by user.\n     */\n    @SuppressLint("UseSparseArrays")\n    private void loadHomeDir() {\n        historyMap = new HashMap<>();\n        historyCounter = 0;\n        String absolutePath = Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory().getAbsolutePath();\n        PrefUtils<String> homeDirPreference = new PrefUtils<>(HOME_DIR);\n        String startDir = homeDirPreference.getValue();\n        if (startDir == null) {\n            homeDirPreference.setValue(absolutePath);\n            startDir = absolutePath;\n        }\n        loadPath(startDir);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * F5\n     * <p>\n     * Keeps the current scroll position since the content of the list doesn\'t change (much)\n     * when simply reloading the directory that is already displayed.\n     */\n    public void reloadCurrentDirectory() {\n        saveScrollPosition();\n        loadPath(currentDirectory);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Saves the current scroll position of the {@link #fileListView} so it can be restored\n     * after the list has been (re-)populated.\n     *\n     * @see #restoreScrollPosition()\n     */\n    private void saveScrollPosition() {\n        if (fileListView != null) {\n            scrollPosition = fileListView.getFirstVisiblePosition();\n            View firstVisibleItem = fileListView.getChildAt(0);\n            scrollOffset = (firstVisibleItem == null) ? 0 : (firstVisibleItem.getTop() - fileListView.getPaddingTop());\n            scrollPositionSaved = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Restores a previously saved scroll position of the {@link #fileListView}.\n     * Does nothing if no scroll position has been saved.\n     *\n     * @see #saveScrollPosition()\n     */\n    private void restoreScrollPosition() {\n        if (scrollPositionSaved) {\n            scrollPositionSaved = false; // only restore once per saved position\n            if (fileListView != null) {\n                final int position = scrollPosition;\n                final int offset = scrollOffset;\n                fileListView.setSelectionFromTop(position, offset);\n                // the adapter has just been set, so the list may not be laid out yet\n                fileListView.post(() -> fileListView.setSelectionFromTop(position, offset));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")\n    // one of the two methods that may call the "deprecated" functions.\n    public void loadArchivePath(String path, FMArchive archive) {\n        loadArchive(path, archive);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Changes directory.\n     *\n     * @param path the path to cd into\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")\n    // one of the two methods that may call the "deprecated" functions.\n    public void loadPath(String path) {\n        ArchiveParentFinder archiveResult = new ArchiveParentFinder(path).invoke();\n        boolean archive = archiveResult.isArchive();\n        FMArchive aF = archiveResult.getArchiveFile();\n        if (archive) {\n            loadArchive(path, aF);\n        } else {\n            loadDirectory(path);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Changes directory.\n     *\n     * @param path directory to load\n     * @deprecated may only be called by {@link #loadPath(String)}\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings("DeprecatedIsStillUsed") // see above\n    private void loadDirectory(String path) {\n        ArrayList<FMFile> files;\n        FileLoader fileLoader = new FileLoader(path);\n        View errorText = findViewById(R.id.unableToLoadText);\n        View emptyText = findViewById(R.id.emptyDirText);\n        try {\n            files = fileLoader.loadLocationFiles();\n            fileListView.setVisibility(VISIBLE);\n            errorText.setVisibility(GONE);\n            emptyText.setVisibility(GONE);\n\n            arrayAdapter = new FileArrayAdapter(this, R.layout.layout_file, sortFilesByPreference(files, new PrefUtils<String>(SORT_FILES_BY).getValue()));\n            fileListView.setAdapter(arrayAdapter);\n            restoreScrollPosition();\n        } catch (NoAccessException e) {\n            Log.w(TAG, "Can\'t read \'" + path + "\': Permission denied!");\n            fileListView.setVisibility(GONE);\n            errorText.setVisibility(VISIBLE);\n            emptyText.setVisibility(GONE);\n        } catch (EmptyDirectoryException e) {\n            Log.w(TAG, "Can\'t read \'" + path + "\': Empty directory!");\n            fileListView.setVisibility(GONE);\n            errorText.setVisibility(GONE);\n            emptyText.setVisibility(VISIBLE);\n        }\n        scrollPositionSaved = false; // don\'t restore an outdated position on a later load\n        currentDirectory = path;\n\n        HistoryEntry entry = historyMap.get(historyCounter);\n        if (entry != null && !entry.getPath().equals(currentDirectory)) {\n            historyMap.put(historyCounter++, new HistoryEntry(currentDirectory, null));\n        }\n        setToolbarText();\n        setFreeSpaceText();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Changes "directory" into an archive.\n     *\n     * @param path archive to load\n     * @deprecated may only be called by {@link #loadPath(String)}\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings("DeprecatedIsStillUsed") // see above\n    private void loadArchive(String path, FMArchive archive) {\n        ArrayList<FMFile> files;\n        ArchiveLoader loader = new ArchiveLoader(archive, path);\n        View errorText = findViewById(R.id.unableToLoadText);\n        View emptyText = findViewById(R.id.emptyDirText);\n\n        files = loader.loadLocationFiles();\n        fileListView.setVisibility(VISIBLE);\n        errorText.setVisibility(GONE);\n        emptyText.setVisibility(GONE);\n\n        arrayAdapter = new ArchiveArrayAdapter(this, R.layout.layout_file, sortFilesByPreference(files, new PrefUtils<String>(SORT_FILES_BY).getValue()), archive);\n        fileListView.setAdapter(arrayAdapter);\n        restoreScrollPosition();\n\n        currentDirectory = path;\n        historyMap.put(historyCounter++, new HistoryEntry(currentDirectory, archive));\n        setToolbarText();\n        setFreeSpaceText();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Sorts files by user preference.\n     *\n     * @param files files to sort\n     * @param pref  chosen preference\n     * @return sorted list\n     */\n    private ArrayList<FMFile> sortFilesByPreference(ArrayList<FMFile> files, String pref) {\n        if (pref.equals(getString(R.string.pref_sortby_value_name))) {\n            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text="## Summary\n\n**Bug:** Marking a file for an operation (copy/move/zip/extract), clearing the op context, or otherwise reloading the current directory made the file list jump back to the top.\n\n**Root cause** — `app/src/main/java/io/lerk/lrkFM/activities/FileActivity.java`:\n\nEvery op-context action (`ContextMenuUtil.addMoveToMenu`/`addCopyToMenu`/`addCreateZipToMenu`, `clearFileOpCache()`, `OperationUtil`, etc.) ends with `reloadCurrentDirectory()`. That method calls `loadPath()` → `loadDirectory()`/`loadArchive()`, which build a **brand-new** `FileArrayAdapter`/`ArchiveArrayAdapter` and call `fileListView.setAdapter(...)`. Attaching a new adapter resets the `ListView`'s scroll state to position 0, so the user was thrown back to the top of the list.\n\n**Fix:**\n\n1. Added three fields tracking the list's scroll state: `scrollPosition` (first visible item index), `scrollOffset` (pixel offset of that item, so restoration is pixel-accurate rather than snapping to an item boundary), and `scrollPositionSaved`.\n2. `reloadCurrentDirectory()` now calls a new `saveScrollPosition()` before `loadPath(currentDirectory)`, capturing `getFirstVisiblePosition()` and the top offset of the first visible child (adjusted for list padding).\n3. Added `restoreScrollPosition()`, invoked right after `setAdapter(...)` in both `loadDirectory()` and `loadArchive()`. It calls `setSelectionFromTop(position, offset)` and repeats it in a `post(...)` runnable, because the list has not been laid out yet at the moment the adapter is set.\n4. The saved state is one-shot and is also cleared at the end of `loadDirectory()`/`loadArchive()`, so a failed reload (`NoAccessException`/`EmptyDirectoryException`) can never leak a stale scroll position into a subsequent navigation to a *different* directory — normal directory changes still start at the top as expected.\n\nThis preserves the scroll position for all reload paths (plain directories and archive browsing) while leaving regular navigation behaviour unchanged. `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` completes with **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**.")], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 698, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 39674, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 698, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYLr62wPznFYVAvmHwKK', stop_reason=None, session_id='0b048210-95c8-49ff-96c6-f8b0660e0603', uuid='390aa008-4016-493b-a9e2-df11493ea97d')
